# Skills · C · part 3

858 entries, ca-afp-eval to cache-hit-debug. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ca-afp-eval**: Evaluates federated learning models for human activity recognition under non-IID data distributions. It measures classification accuracy, fairness across heterogeneous clients, and communication efficiency during model pruning and clustering. Use when the user wants to benchmark on WISDM, UCI-HAR, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/ca-afp-eval) · ★3)
- **ca-agency-primer**: Build the background briefing that lets a rep understand a California state agency as a system rather than one facility — what the department does, how its sites differ, where the money is, and where Black Box Safety stands at each location. Use when a rep is taking on an agency or preparing for a first meeting inside a multi-site department, or asks what an agency is, what its facilities do, how many sites it has, why one site buys differently from another, or where the whitespace is across CDCR, DSH, CalVet, Caltrans, Cal Fire, CHP or DGS. Use it too for narrower asks — "tell me about Patto… ([Black-Box-Safety/bbs-claude-marketplace](https://github.com/Black-Box-Safety/bbs-claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/bbs-sales/skills/ca-agency-primer))
- **ca-agent**: Indian Chartered Accountant (CA) agent for companies, LLPs, firms and individuals. Maintains double-entry books (vouchers, ledgers, trial balance, bank reconciliation, party master, debtors/creditors ageing, party-wise advances, balance confirmations, year-end close), prepares GST returns (GSTR-1, GSTR-3B with rule 88A ITC set-off, GSTR-2B reconciliation, GSTR-9 working, interest and late fee), computes income tax and prepares ITR data (ITR-6 for companies with 115BAA/normal/MAT, ITR-1 to ITR-5, old vs new regime, advance tax, TDS, interest u/s 234A/B/C), and drafts Schedule III balance sheet… ([magnetomohit-jpg/ca-agent-skill](https://github.com/magnetomohit-jpg/ca-agent-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/ca-agent))
- **ca-agent-builder**: Standard och mall för att bygga och driftsätta AI-agent-system åt kunder (prompt.md, tools.yaml, config.yaml). Använd denna skill vid utveckling, testning och driftsättning av klientagenter. ([dopaminedotmd/styde](https://github.com/dopaminedotmd/styde/tree/HEAD/skills/delivery/ca-agent-builder))
- **ca-architecture-boundaries**: Applies Clean Architecture's dependency-direction and SRP-by-actor rules to system-level boundary design: separates business logic from infrastructure, identifies actor-coupling risks, and enforces inward-pointing dependency arrows. For system scope (multiple components, layers, or services), not routine or module scope (use cc-routine-and-class-design). ([ryanthedev/code-foundations](https://github.com/ryanthedev/code-foundations/tree/HEAD/skills/ca-architecture-boundaries) · ★390)
- **ca-arrive**: Quelques jours avant vos règles, prépare pour la personne qui partage votre vie un mot que vous relisez et envoyez vous-même. À lancer seulement quand on l'appelle par son nom. ([ludovicsanchez38-creator/ca-arrive](https://github.com/ludovicsanchez38-creator/ca-arrive/tree/HEAD/ca-arrive))
- **ca-ask-claude-review**: Ask a fresh Claude session to review local changes together with the branch diff from the default branch. Use when the user asks for a Claude review, another model's perspective, or an independent review after ordinary implementation. Works without a plan, PR, previous conversation, or ca implementation loop. ([ken2403/agent-parallel-dev-plugin](https://github.com/ken2403/agent-parallel-dev-plugin/tree/HEAD/ca/codex/skills/ca-ask-claude-review) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **ca-audience**: Use this skill when someone wants to know which customers a brand is winning or losing by age: how its spend splits across generations, how that mix is moving, and how it compares with a competitor's, even if they only ask about Gen Z or younger customers. It reads card spend by generation, adjusted for the panel's age skew, and income or gender where the data covers them. Not for a musician's or actor's fans. For a full report, use this package's report skill. ([Carbon-Arc/carbon-arc-skills](https://github.com/Carbon-Arc/carbon-arc-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/carbon-arc-company-intelligence/skills/ca-audience) · ★17)

- **ca-audit-agent**: Genomför kundaudits för att kartlägga IT-flöden, system, kostnader och identifiera automationsmöjligheter. Används för att samla in strukturerad data om kundens processer. ([dopaminedotmd/styde](https://github.com/dopaminedotmd/styde/tree/HEAD/skills/delivery/ca-audit-agent))

- **ca-benchmark**: Use this skill when someone wants to know whether a consumer brand's move is its own or the whole category's: who is winning or losing share, how it compares with named rivals, and whether it is selling less or selling cheaper, even if they only ask why their numbers are down.
